<!-- page 1 -->
NAVAJO COUNTY BOARD OF SUPERVISORS’ MINUTES 
Tuesday, September 23, 2014 
 
PRESENT: Jonathan M. Nez, Supervisor; Dawnafe Whitesinger, Supervisor; and Jesse Thompson, Chairman 
were present. David Tenney, Supervisor was absent.  
9:09 a.m.  Call To Order: Pledge of Allegiance and Invocation led by Supervisor Whitesinger.  
CALL TO THE PUBLIC: Terry Hill expressed his gratitude to Sylvia Allen for the time that she served on the 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors. Tom Porcharsky expressed his gratitude to Sylvia Allen for the tim e that 
she served on the Board. Steve Slaton thanked Sylvia Allen for her service and indicated that he is applying for 
the seat on the Navajo County Board of Supervisors.  
      CONSENT AGENDA: a) Warrant & Voucher Report over $1000 August 2014 b) Constable Reports: Holbrook 
Precinct #1, July; Snowflake Precinct #3, August; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, August 2014 c) Juvenile 
Probation Report, August 2014 d) Clerk of Superior Court Report, August 2014 e) Justice Court Reports: 
Holbrook Precinct #1, August; Winslow Precinct #2, August; Snowflake Precinct #3, August; Kayenta Precinct 
#4, August; Show L ow Precinct #5, August; and Pinetop -Lakeside Precinct #6, August 2014 f) Authorize the 
Arizona Attorney General’s Office to represent Navajo County in the pending tax appeal in Novo Bio Power v. 
Arizona Department of Revenue & Navajo County, TX-201300327 and related litigation g) Department of Public 
Safety Memorandum of Understanding 2014-172 between the Sheriff’s Office and State of Arizona, Department 
of Public Safety for $17,300 to be used pursuant to A.R.S. §41 -1724 h) Adopt Resolution Number 21 -14, 
cancelling the election for the NPC Community College District Governing Board i) Adopt Resolution Number 
22-14, cancelling the elections for the Clay Springs DWID, Wonderland Acres DWID, Porter Creek DWID, 
Joseph City DWID, Woodruff DWID, Heber DWID, Misty Mountain DWID, Overgaard Townsite DWID, and 
the Three -O-Three DWID j) Adopt Resolution Number 23 -14, cancelling the election for the Clay 
Springs/Pinedale Fire Dist., McLaws Rd. Fire Dist., Joseph City Fire Dist., and Woodruff Fire District k) Adopt 
Resolution Number 24 -14, cancelling the election for the Navapache Hospital District l) Adopt Resolution 
Number 25-14, cancelling the election for the Joseph City Sanitary Dist., Pinetop -Lakeside Sanitary Dist., and 
Heber/Overgaard Sanitary District m) Adopt Resolution Number 26-14, cancelling the election for Joseph City 
USD #2, Holbrook USD #3 (Ward 1 and 2), Pinon USD #4, Snowflake USD #5, Heber/Overgaard USD #6, 
Whiteriver USD #20, Cedar USD #25, NAVIT  Districts – Winslow USD #1, Joseph City USD #2, Holbrook  
USD #3, Heber/Overgaard USD #6, Show Low USD #10, Whiteriver USD #20 and Blue Ridge USD #32, 
NATIVE Districts – Pinon USD #4 and Kayenta USD #27 n) Installation of the STOP signs on Constellation Cir 
and Constellation Lane in Mogollon Airpark o) Modifications to Attachment “C” to the Master Intergovernmental 
Agreement (IGA) CO10070 with the Navajo Nation and Navajo County for Road Project p) Contract signed by 
County Manager per Board of Supervisors Resolution: Ironside Engineering & Development, Inc. Authorization 
of Services for Stone Bridge Trail q) Navajo County comments on the Proposed Revision to the Nonessential 
Experimental Population of the Mexican Wolf. Federal Register / Vol. 79, No. 143 / 43358 / Friday, July 25, 2014 
AND Draft Environmental Imp act Statement for the Proposed Revision to the Nonessential Experimental 
Population of the Mexican Wolf (Canis Lupus Baileyi), July 16, 2014 r) Letter to the Secretary of the Interior and 
the Director of U.S. Fish and Wildlife Service regarding lack of compliance of the U.S. Fish and Wildlife Service 
Proposed Revision of the Nonessential Experimental Population of the Mexican Wolf process with the 
requirements of ESA under 50 CFR § 17.81 (d) s) Sympathy letter to the Honorable Dwight Witherspoon and  
family: Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to authorize the Chairman to sign the items in the consent 
agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 2 -->
EMPLOYEE RECOGNITION: Navajo County Recognizes Stephanie Wofford, Probation; Brandon 
Andrews, Public Works; and Codi Starks, Information Technology for their excellence in personal performance 
far exceeding organization expectations: Kimberly Eavenson recognized Stephanie Wofford; Brandon Andrews 
and Codi Starks for their outstanding performance to Navajo County. A short video of the employee recognition 
was shown. Mrs. Eavenson thanked each of the employees and honored them with a certificate of recognition, a 
$50.00 gift card and a leaf to put on the employee r ecognition tree. Chairman Thompson thanked all of the 
employees for their hard work and dedication to Navajo County.  
BOARD OF SUPERVISORS:  
a. Presentation to Mrs. Sylvia Allen for her service to Navajo County : Chairman Thompson  made a 
presentation to former Supervisor of District III, Sylvia Allen. Chairman Thompson stated how thankful 
we are for her work she did as Supervisor.  Mrs. Allen thanked the Board for their kindness and goodness. 
She thanked all of the employees, Melissa, Debra, Catrina and Sheila.  She thanked Mr. Jayne. She 
indicated that life can change on a dime and she will do her best on the Senate. Supervisor Whitesinger 
thanked her friend Sylvia Allen for being so kind taking her under her wing.  She  wished her the best of 
the luck. Supervisor Nez expressed his appreciation for her insight and he feels confident that she will be 
our senator and we will have a great champion for this area. Mr. Jayne expressed his appreciation to Mrs. 
Allen during their time working together.  Brad Carlyon thanked Sylvia and indicated that he will be in 
contact. Chairman Thompson expressed his appreciation from former Supervisor Percy Deal.  
b. Consideration and possible selection from the proposed list of three registered Republicans who reside in 
Navajo County to fill the vacant Senate Seat in District 6 as received from the Executive Director of the 
Arizona Republican Party : Brad Carlyon discussed the item. He indicated that the Republican Party 
proposed a list of three registered Republicans; they have requested that this board appoint the widow of 
Chester Crandell, Alice Crandell. Supervisor Nez made a motion  to appoint Alice Crandell to fill the 
vacant Senate Seat in Legislative District No. 6; motion was seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion. Alice Crandell addressed the Board stating what an honor it is to serve 
the remaining term . She spoke of her husband’s service  and hopes that she can do what she can while 
holding the position . Sylvia Allen expressed her support for Senator Alice Crandell. Chairman 
Thompson expressed his thoughts about former Senator Crandell.  
c. BOARD OF SUPERVISORS ACTING AS THE BOARD OF EQUALIZATION: Consideration and 
possible approval of the Board of Equalization Hearing Officer’s recommendation on hearings conducted 
on September 18, 2014 for Petition for Review of Real Property Valuation : Supervisor Nez made a  
motion to enter into the Board of Equalization session; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. 
Vote unanimous approving the motion. Melissa Buckley discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger 
made a motion  to approve the Board of Equalization Hearing Officer’s recommendation on hearings 
conducted on September 18, 2014 for Petition for Review of Real Prop erty Valuation; motion seconded 
by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to 
return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
 
 
 
HUMAN RESOURCES:  
a. Consideration and possible approval of Personnel Actions : Kimberly Eavenson reviewed the 17 item 
personnel action report. Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve the personnel actions as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.

<!-- page 3 -->
b. Presentation to retiring employee Roger Kramer : Eric Scott recognized Roger Kramer fo r his service to 
Navajo County.   
NAVAJO COUNTY PUBLIC HEALTH SERVICES DISTRICT: Board of Directors Session: Supervisor 
Nez made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Public Health Services District Board of Directors Session; 
motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
a. CONSENT AGENDA:   
i) Contract #ADHS15 -078234 with the Arizona Department of Heal th Services for the Oral Health 
Dental Sealant Program for October 1, 2014 to September 30, 2019:  
ii) Contract #ADHS14 -071223 with the Arizona Department of Health Service for the Sexual 
Transmitted Disease (STD) Program for January 1, 2014 to December 31, 2019 in the amount of 
$7,557.00: 
iii) Amendment #1 of Contract #ADHS14 -053056 with Arizona Department of Health Services for the 
WIC Program for October 1, 2014 to September 30, 2015 in the amount of $344,996.00: 
Mary Herring answered questions of the Board. Supervisor Nez made a motion  to approve  the 
consent agenda as presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
b. Presentation on Public Health Accreditation : Mary Herring gave a brief presentation. Chairman 
Thompson made comments. Mr. Jayne indicated that this is a great example of representing Navajo 
County.  Mary Herring clarified the timeline  for Supervisor Whitesinger . Mary Herring answered 
questions of the Board.  
c. Consideration and possible approval of a Professional Services Agreement with Lee Haggenmuller, RDH 
for Dental Hygiene services through the Arizona First Things First, Navajo Nation, Oral Health Fluoride 
Program effective September 1, 2014 to June 30, 2015 in the amount of $45.00/per hour : Mary Herring 
discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger  made a motion  to approve the Professional Services 
Agreement with Lee Haggenmuller, RDH for Dental Hygiene services through the Arizona First Things 
First, Navajo Nation, Oral Health Fluoride Program effective Septe mber 1, 2014 to June 30, 2015 in the 
amount of $45.00/per hour; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
d. Consideration and possible approval of a Memorandum of Understanding with the Winslow Indian Health 
Care Center to establish the terms, conditions and responsibilities for the deployment, receipt, storage and 
distribution of medical countermeasures to the servi ce area of 638 institution: Mary Herring and John 
Zimmerman discussed the item. Chairman Thompson made comments. Supervisor Whitesinger made 
a motion to approve the Memorandum of Understanding with the Winslow Indian Health Care Center to 
establish the terms, conditions and responsibilities for the deployment, receipt, storage and distribution of 
medical countermeasures to the service area of 638 institution; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote 
unanimous approving the motion.  
Supervisor Nez made a motion  to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
COUNTY ATTORNEY:  
a. Consideration and possible acceptance of the Attorney General’s FY15 Victims’ Rights Program Grant 
Agreement for A.G. #:2015-0010 in the amount of $41,000: Catrina Roe discussed the item. Supervisor 
Nez made a motion to approve the Attorney General’s FY15 Victims’ Rights Program Grant Agreement 
for A.G. #:2015 -0010 in the amount of $41,000 ; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote  
unanimous approving the motion.  
b. Consideration and possible approval of a Memoran dum of Understanding with Alice’s Place  to provide 
services: Catrina Roe discussed the item. Supervisor Whitesinger  made a motion  to approve the

<!-- page 4 -->
Memorandum of Understanding with Alice’s Place to provide services; motion seconded by  Supervisor 
Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
      EMERGENCY MANAGEMENT: Consideration and possible approval of FY2014 Homeland Security Grant 
Program Awards : Catrina Roe discussed the item. Supervisor Nez  made a motion  to approve  the FY2014 
Homeland Security Grant Program Awards ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
PUBLIC WORKS:  
NAVAJO COUNTY FLOOD CONTROL DISTRICT: Board of Directors Session: CONSENT  
AGENDA: Supervisor Nez made a motion to enter into the Navajo County Flood Control District Board 
of Directors Session ; motion seconded by  Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the 
motion. Consideration and possible approval of the Community Rating System Recertification Letter : 
Homero Vela discussed the item . Supervisor Nez made a motion  to approve the consent agenda as 
presented; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger . Vote unanimous approving the motion.   
Supervisor Nez made a motion  to return to regular session; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion.  
c. Consideration and possible approval to award Contract #B14 -07-025 Supply of Aggregate for Cover 
Material to the lowest responsible bidder Brimhall Sand and Rock and Building Materials, Inc.: Homero 
Vela discussed the item.  Supervisor Whitesinger made a motion to approve the Contract #B14-07-025 
Supply of Aggregate for C over Material to the lowest responsible bidder Brimhall Sand and Rock and 
Building Materials, Inc.; motion seconded by Supervisor Nez. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
      PUBLIC WORKS/PLANNING & ZONING: Consideration and possible adoption of the following 
Resolutions, approving the Special Use Permits , time extensions for Met eorological Towers : Trent Larson 
discussed the item and answered questions of the Board. The applicant, Betsey made some brief comments.  
a. Resolution Number 27-14 located on Parcel Number 202-01-005D 
b. Resolution Number 28-14 located on Parcel Number 202-01-005C 
c. Resolution Number 29-14 located on Parcel Number 202-26-008 
d. Resolution Number 30-14 located on Parcel Number 201-13-001 
e. Resolution Number 31-14 located on Parcel Number 201-13-004 
Supervisor Nez made a motion  to adopt Resolution Number 27-14 located on Parcel Number 202 -01-005D; 
Resolution Number 28-14 located on Parcel Number 202-01-005C; Resolution Number 29-14 located on Parcel 
Number 202-26-008; Resolution Number 30 -14 located on Parcel Number 201 -13-001; Resolution Number 
31-14 located on Parcel Number 201 -13-004; motion seconded by Supervisor Whitesinger. Vote unanimous 
approving the motion.  
 
At 10:35 a.m. Supervisor Nez left the Board of Supervisor meeting.  
      ARIZONA ASSOCIATION OF COUNTIES : AACo Presentation 2014: Cammy Darris introduced Jen 
Marson, the Executive Director of AACo  and presented her with Navajo County coasters and a Navajo County 
binder. Mrs. Marson gave a brief presentation from AACo. Caleb Lennon spoke about the Nationwide 457 Plan. 
Mr. Jayne thanked Caleb for being so visible to our employees. Mrs. Marson continued with the presentation 
from AACo. Chairman Thompson thanked Mrs. Marson for the work that she does as well as AACo. Mr. Jayne 
thanked Jen for being here and made commen ts about the partnership between Navajo County and AACo. 
Chairman Thompson made comments.

<!-- page 5 -->
COUNTY BUSINESS UPDATE : Report from Board members, County Attorney and County Manager : 
Supervisor Whitesinger reported on conversation happening on September 29 regarding opportunities for those 
investing in good neighbors. Chairman Thompson reported on Dilkon and Teesto Chapter buildings that burned 
down.  He r eported on the Dilkon fair and rotary club  and commented that they have received a lot of support 
from ADOT.   
      ADJOURN: At 11:21 p.m. Chairman Thompson made a motion to adjourn; motion seconded by Supervisor 
Whitesinger. Vote unanimous approving the motion. 
 
APPROVED:       DATED: October 14, 2014 
 
Jesse Thompson, Chairman 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors 
 
 
ATTEST: 
 
Melissa W. Buckley, Clerk 
Navajo County Board of Supervisors
